Episode 1⃣
            ‼Abdulbaseet ‼
Na : Bintnagz( Hasna mansur nagoda)
Check out on wattpad @bintnags

Ruwan sama ake tafkawa kamar da bakin kwarya titin azman yayi tsit babu ko mota daya da take shigewa bare keke, sauri take kamar zata tashi sama duk da ruwan na dukan ta ama ta kwamace ta ci gaba da tafiya ko allah ya temake ta ta sami abun hawa tana cikin tafiya, abun tun baya bata tsoro abun har ya fara bata tsoro chan ta fara jero yan adduoin ta da azkar dinta ,su suka dauke mata hankali ta cigaba da tafiya
   Giffffffffffff kake ji mota tayi kanta kasan cewar motar a guje ta taho dan ko burki bana kyautata zaton yanayi a motar nan.... hummer jeep ce sabuwa dal da ita.... shiru kake ji har wajen minti biyar ba'a fito duba abun da aka kade ba sai chan kuma aka fito daga motar, wani dogon saurayi ne sanye yake da hoody da wando da pcap duka bakake sai 👟 👟 takalma sa kuma farare ya zuba uban  bakin gilashi da ya kika masa fuska.
   Ya karaso wajen da take, ya kali gabas da yamma kudu da arewa babu wanda yake gilmawa ta wajen sai uban ruwan saman da ke sauka a kansu.... cicibarta yayi ya sanya ta bayan motarsa kasan cewa a sume take saidai ba jini ko daya da yake fita daga jikin ta,  sannan ya taka motar sa yayi ya bar gurin 
  Kai tsaye bayero clinic ya kaita cikin hanzari suka karbe ta dan ganin irin yanayi da take ciki
Nan kallo ya koma kan sa dan yadda yake haka ya siga clinic din kamar wani shedan ya sha babken kaya daga samar har kasa gannin kallon yayi masa yawa yasa ya zari face mask ya saka a fuskar sa shima kuma baki....nan likitan da zai duba ta ya karaso ya ke tambayar shi abun da ya faru? Sai da ya gama yan muzuran sa sanna ya amsa cikin dashashiyar murua sai kace wanda ya shekara bai yi magana ba🤐 
" a hanya na tsince ta a bakin titi da alama mota ce ta bige ta🙄" ya fada cikin harshe turanci. 
Nan Likita ya dora masa allamun tambaya dan tunda ya shigo da ita shi bai yarda da shi ba da gani wannan mutumun ba kalau yake ba dan bai yi kama da wanda za'a yarda da shi ba.... nan likitan ya ce masa gskia idan yana so a duba ta sai ya kawo police report ko kuma ya dauke ta ya kaita general hospital inda zata sami cikakiyar kulawa inda kuma akwai kwararun likitoci
 Nurse ce tashigo office din likitan da gudu har tana haki tace DR parient din da ya kawo tana convulsing (jijiga) tana bukatar kulawar gagawa a guje shi ma ya fita likitan
Cikin dan kankanin lokaci suka tsaida jijigar suka saka mata ruwa nan likitan ya bawa nurses yan gwaje gwajen da Xrays din da  za'ayi mata sannan ya koma 
Yana zaune inda yabar shi, chan wani tunani yazo wa likitan yace rayuwar wannan baiwar allahn yakamata na fara cetowa kafin na ji da wannan bawan allahn 
Yanda likita bai kula shi ba shima bai kula likitan ba har nurses suka shigo da sakamakin gwanjin da aka yo mata, likita ya kalla yace Alhmdulillah jin haka yayi sauri ya dago ya kalli likitan, sakamako yanuna normal kawai dai zafin buguwa ne ya sa mata cerebral contusions, mun bata magun guna da karin ruwa da za'ayi mata zata farfado akowane  lokaci daga yanzu, ga wannan kaje ka biya bill
   Har cikin zuciyar sa ya ji dadin jin ba wani babban abu ne ya same ta ba hakan yasa yace Thank you Dr ya dauki takardun ya fice 
   Bayan yaje ya biya kudin sannan ya koma dakin da aka kwantar da ita 
Sai a lokacin ya kalli fiskar ta dan ko da ya dako ta bai kalli fuskar ta ba............farace tassss dan duk inda ake irga fararen mutane to za'a sako ta a ciki, kasan cewar kanta ba dankwali hakan yasa gashin ta duk ya zubo kan fuskar ta da wuyan ta ya rufe hakan yasa bai wani tantance ta ba, gannin kafafunta santala santala a waje yasa ya ja mata zanin ta zuwa kasa.... wani abun hannu da ya makala a hannun sa mai kararawa ya dan buga kasancewar yayi motsi da hannun wajen rufe mata kafa, a hankali ta bude idon ta tana neman inda ta jiyo sautin, babu abun da idon ta  yakai ga  gannin sai hannun sa mai awarwaron kararawao, daga ido tayi taga fuskar sa amma fusar a rufe yasha baki kamar kidnapper hakan ya bata tsoro da sauri ta rufe ido ta fara jero addu'oin tsari. Ji tayi ya dau wayar ta da aka ajiye mata a gefen gadon. Message yayi composing ya duba contacts dinta ya nemo mama ko mommy ama duk bai gani ba, chan yaga ansa yaya Ado, nan ya tuno kalamar yaya da abun da take nufi hakan yasa ya tura wa yaya ado message din sannan ya ajiye mata wayar dadai kanta sannan ya fice, ta bude ido a hankali ta bi bayansa da kallo
 Lafiyar wannan mutun kuwa toh meye hakan  anya kuwa wannan dan musulmi ne???? Wannan tambayoyin tai ta jera wa kanta har taji shigowar yan gidan su dakin.....
Umman ta ce da yaya Ado suka shigo dakin tare da likita nan likitan ya kwashe labarin abin da ya faru daga zuwan su asibitin har yanzu ya kuma zari dubi dari cash yace wannan ne kudin da ya bayar kafin ya tafi....
To me zasu ce 
Godiya ko me zasu yi wa wannan bawan allahn??????

[11/16, 8:46 AM] Aysha Ado Albasu: Episode 2⃣
          ‼Abdulbaseet ‼
Na : Bintnagz( Hasna Mansur nagoda)
Check out on wattpad @bintnags

Salama yarinya ce yar shekara ashirin farace tasss san kowa kin wanda ya rasa ga nustuwa ga kamala ga hankali ga kunya ga ilimin boka da na addini , duk inda ake lisafo  mata na gari to salama ta kai wajen. Makaranta da makota kowa kwatance yake da salama , har mutane da yawa kan ce hakikanin gaskia ta ci sunan ta SALAMA saboda tsabar hakurin ta da kuma salamar ta.
Su biyu iyayyen ta suka haifa ita da yayan ta yaya Ado wanda mahaifiyar su ke rikon su , tun salama na da shekara biyar a duniya allah ya dauki ran baban su salama wato mallan muhammadu , haka mahaifiyar su ta dau kaddara ta cigaba da rainon yayan ta har suka girma ba tare da ta sake yin wani auren ba.
 Salama yan asalin garin adamawa ne dan a chan aka haifesu ita da yaya Ado fulani ne na hakika dan ko hausar su bata fita sosai har yanzu, kasan cewar uban su da ya mutu bai bar musu komai ba hakan yasa mahaifiyar su  ta kuduri niyar sai ta ci moriyar yaranta guda biyu da allah ya mallaka mata aduniya,  saida mahaifinsu ya rasu sannan  Bodado ta debo yaran ta daga adamawa zuwa garin kano domin neman kudi da sama  wa yaran ta illimi ingantace....
Hakan nan sukayi ta gumurzu yau akwai gobe babu ita da yaran ta , babu sana'ar da batayi ba yan kullin kayan miya ne yan provisions ne da take siyar wa daga gida har allah ya temaketa ya buda mata ta samu ta malaki gidan kanta ciki da pallo sai kuma kitchen da bandaki, haka ta dage ta sa yaran ta a makaranta, mutane da dama sun zo neman auren Bodado amma kasan cewar ita a rayuwar ta babu abun da ya fi mata mahimmanci idan ba yayan ta biyu ba hakan yasa duk tayi watsi da su.
Kwanci tashi kamar baza akai inda ake ba yanzu allah ya buda wa bodado domin a yanzu kudi ya zauna mata, atamfofi leshi da material da sauran kayan mata babu wanda bata siyar wa,hakan yasa mata ke cikowa a gidan ta. 
Yaya ado kuma da salama duk sun gama makanranta, yaya ado economics ya karanta yanzu haka har ya gama service yana jiran aiki, ita kuma salama ta gama secondary kennan ta sami admission a Buk ta fara agriculture, yanzu haka tana aji daya.
**********************************************************************************************************
Ko da ya fito daga asibitin kai tsaye gida ya nufa dan ruwan ya tsagaita duk da ya bige salama hakan bai sa nutsuwa tazo masa ba na ya rage gudu innaaaaa take motar yake yi kamar zai tashi zasa, ga uban music din da ya saki a motar sai dum dum dum take yi kamar zata fashe 🙄
Horn yayi a bakin wani katafaren gida maigadi da sauri ya zo bude masa gate din maigadin ya sunkuyar da kai dan gaishe shi , ina shi bai ma san yana yi ba dan gilas din motar tinted ne, da gudu ya sheka yaje parking lot ya ajiye motar ya fito ya sa kai cikin wani apartment dake gefe, hannun sa yasa ya bude dayake kofar bangaren nasa da thumbprint dinsa kadai take aiki kofar ta bude ya sa kai ta rufe da kanta.
Yana shi toilet yayi, yayi wanka ya fito har zai haye gado sai kuma ya chanza shawara ta tashi yayi fridge ya dako red wine 🍷 ya zuba a glass 🥃 ya sami guri ya zauna bayan ya kunna sauti mai dukan kunne.
 Hakanan ya zauna kurum ba abun da yakeyi bayan  gyada kan da yakeyi yana bin sautin wakar haka har aka fara kiraye kirayen sallar magariba , kasancewar ya bugu ya kasa tashi anan ya bige da bacci..... bai tashi ba sai karfe tara da rabi shima wayarsa ce tayi ta ringing hakan yasa ya tashi..... bosss ya gani na kiran sa a waya, yayi gyaran murya ya dauka, TOH kawai naji yace ya tashi ya zura riga a jikinsa sannan ya fita.
 Wani katafaren fallo ya shiga, ya ji kayan alatu da kayan na'ura a fallon da kujeru na alfarma.
Kamar ka shiga masarautar sarkin saudiya, waje ya samu ya zauna a kasa kusa da wasu yan mata guda biyu, sama kuma kan kujera Alhaji ne da hajiyar sa a zaune sun hakince.
 Alhajin yayi gyaran murya ya kalli daya dagacikin yan matan yace Naima yaushe kika ce zaki koma makaranta , ta dago tace daddy this coming satuday inshaallah, kinyi confirming jirgin da komai da komai, eh dadi nayi emirate airline ne 12 na dare zai tashi so zanje airport wajen 10 kenan,Toh allah ya temaka allah ya kuma bada sa'a, amin daddy ta fada yayin da ya maida kallon sa zuwa ga hajiya....... hajiya ina ganin zan hada hajar da  na'ima waje daya tunda hajar yanzu tana kan gama Weac dinta idan yaso sai na tura ta france 🇫🇷 ita ma ta karanta law din...... nan dai suka yi ta shawarwari kan karatun naima da hajar sai da suka gama tataunawa sannan suka karaso kan shi...
Fuskar alhaji ba ko murmushi sam ba kamar yanda yayi wa na'ima da hajar magana ba yace kai Abdulbaseet ya zaro ATM card guda biyu ya bashi yasa hannu biyu ya karba tare da fadin Thank you daddy, ko kallo bai ishe shi ba balle ya juyo ya kalleshi kawai yace tashi ka fita ka bamu waje....sum sum da shi ya tashi ya fita, nan kuma ya juya ya cigaba da hira da su naima da hajar da kuma mamar su harda dariya.
***************************************************

[11/16, 8:46 AM] Aysha Ado Albasu: Episode 3⃣
        ‼ABDULBASEET ‼
Na :bintnagz( Hasna mansur nagoda)
Check out on wattpad @bintnagz

Yau kwana biyu kennan da salamar salama daga asibiti dan washe gari aka sallamo ta. 
              ****************************
Figib ta tashi daga baccin da takeyi tana haki karar kararawar bracelet din abdulbaseet ce ta cika mata kunne cikin mafarkin ta hakan yasa ta tashi a firgice, ta tashi tana salati ba shiri ta tashi tayi alwala tayi nafila, nan ta dan shingida ta fara tunanin gayen da ta gani a asibiti dan tun da ta dawo ya barta da assignment dan bata da wani tunani face na yarda zata san meya faru da ita har ya dauke ta ya kaita asibiti, kuma wanene shi??? Sake saken da take tayi kenna, Har bacci ya kwashe ta. 
Karfe shida  na safe ta tashi bayan ta kara farkawa tayi sallahar asuba, ta gyara musu dakin da yake ita da umman su take kwana ta je tayi musu breakfast sannan ta shiga wanka dan tana da lectures karfe takwas... data fito me take tambaryar umman ta idan ta taso daga makaranta yau zanta biya ta gidan su sabeeha ta dubo ta dan itama bata jin dadi, nan ta amince mata har ta bata sakon atamfar da maman su sabeeha tace tanaso a ajiye mata, ba musu ta dauka ta saka a jaka ta zura takalmi a hanyar fita ta ci karo da yaya ado: har kin fito tace, tace ehh yaya ina kwana ya amsa da lafiya lau yayin da yake laluba aljihun sa yajiyo mukulin mashin dinsa yace muje na kai ki naga kin kusa makara, cikin jin dadi da murna tace toh yaya😁 dan ya hutashe ta kudin mota.
     Ta ta fito daga makaranta kenann ta tsaya gidan su sabeeha, tana siga ta tarar da sabeeha bakin famfo tana alwala......
Auuuu ashe ma jikin da sauki tunda har gaki bakin famfo kina wasan ruwa , tayi murmushi tasce kai salama alwala fa nake yi, nan ta ajiye jakarta ta cire mayafinta ita ma ta hau yin alwala domin gabatar da sallar la'asar
Sai da suka idar salama take cemata ina maman ku, tafita ama yanzu zata dawo ba jimawa zatayi ba ta fada tana linke daddumar da sukayi sallah.
 Sabeeha kin kuwa san me ya faru dani kwana biyu da bakije makaranta ba, ta zaro ido tace me ya faru kuma???
Hmmmm da saidai kawai azo ace miki na mutu wllh, meye haka kuma salama wane irin mutuwa ana zaune kallau??
Ina ranar da baki zo makaranta ba ta ranar monday ranar da akayi wannan uban ruwan da ya zubar da gidaje: ehh sabeeha ta amsa...
Ranar to na fito daga makaranta kennan ina ta tafiya ko zan sami abun hawa ina ta zuba sauri kinsan hanyar azman yarda take shiru ba kowa wllh sabeeha sai farkawa bayi na ganni akan gado asibiti😳
Ido sabeeha ta zuba mata garin yaya meya faru??? Wai wani ne ya kade ni shi kuma wani ya tsince ni ya kaini asibiti
Nan da nan sabeeha ta fara tataba salama tana jujuya ta baki dai ji ciwo ba ko salama ina kika ji ciwo???
Dariya tayi tace ke ba abun da ya same ni wllh kawai dai buguwa ce da shock ya sani suma ama lafiya ta kalau😊
Nan dai ta kwashe labarin wannan bawan allah  ta bata har da kudin da ya barmusu.
Ke salama wllh gayen nan ban yarda da shi ba dayake shi kadai aka saukarwa da imani aduniya daga gannin ki kawai sai ya dauke ki ya kai ki asibiti dan dai shi yafi kowa ,sannan kince yasha bakaken kaya karmar shedan anya kuwa ba dan mafiya bane ba salama ko kuma dan fashi, a dalilin me zai saka bakaken kaya harda rufe fuska da bakin abu idan ba rashi gskia ba ga kararawa a hannu kamar dan pastor,haba salama haba wllh ni ban yarda da shi ba wllh sai kinyi ta addu'a ,waya sani ma ko yana sane ya kade ki ko wani yasa shi....
Salama tayi dariya tace haba sabeeha ni kuwa me nayi wa wani da zai sa a kade ni in banda abunki, koma meye sabeeha ni banga lefin sa ba tunda bai barni a inda nake ba mota tabi takaina na mutu na atleast alhmdulillah, mtsssswww kinji ai halinki salama wannan hakurin naki shi zai kai ki ya baro ki
Umm ni dai kinga ga wanna ki bawa mama ni zan tafi naga garin ya fara duhu, kar ki damu kuma kin san ina addu'a isha alllahu na fi karfin koma meye, koma waye shi, tayi murmushi ta tashi ta yafa maya finta, ta raka ta har bakin kofa sannan ta koma.
Har ta tsare dan sahu ta fada musu sunnan unguwar su, sai kuma ta tuna umman su ta bata list din kayan cefane tun wancen satin kuma bata siyo ba hakan yasa tace wa dan sahun ya kaita sahad din zoorood kawai nan sukai ciniki ta hau ya kaita har bakin sahad ya ajiye ta. 
Tana ciki yin shopping 🛒 dinta tazo zaro taliya , taliya tazo zata rikito mata aka gaba daya kasancewar tsawon ta bai kai ba sai da tayi dage, kara tayi saurin yi tare da dafe kai, cikin hanzari yazo yayi saurin dafe sauran taliyar har saida bell 🛎 din hannun sa yayi kara, karab taji karar da tade tana ji a mafarkin ta, karar da take son allah ya kada hada ta da mai ita, tayi saurin dagowa ta kalle shi cikin hanzari. Hakan kuwa ta kara faruwa yau bakaken kayan ne a jikin sa saidai kuwa yau bai rufe fuska ba saidai uban gilashin da ya fi fuskar tasa girma, kuma yau bai sa pcap ba kansa a bude yake. Babu abun da ta gani face hancin sa dan siriri dogo da glass din yayi tsab akanshi da kuma lips 👄 dinshi sai farar fuskar sa da sajen sa da yayi masa kyau, kafin ta karaso ga kallon hannun sa daya daga cikin ma'aikatan wajen sun karaso wajen sun tare shi kuma Abdulbaseet ya bar wajen..............

[11/16, 8:46 AM] Aysha Ado Albasu: Episode 4⃣
            ‼Abdulbaseet‼
Na: Bintnagz( Hasna mansur nagoda)
Check out on wattpad @bintnags

Hakikanin gskia wannan shine wanda salama ke nema......jiki a sanyaye ta gyara tsayuwarta, bata ma karasa siyan abun da zata siya ba taji ita gida kawai take son tafiya hakan yasa ta dau kayan taje counter wajen biyan kudi, Dubu 6700 haka aka buga mata lissafin ta ta sa hannun tana lalubar kudi amma bata jiyo suba, nan da nan wani gumi ya keto wa salama da tayi saurin sharewa, ta bude wallet dinta ta zazage jakarta ama shiru babu ko sisi inba naira 10 da mai adedeta sahu ya bata ba dazu Inallilahi ta fada a fili nan abun kallo ya koma kanta ta rasa inda zata sa kanta, ga kaya ta jido a hakan ma bata gama siyaya ba yanzu ya zatayi da wannan abun kunyar.
Ganin yarda mutane suka bita da kallo ya janyo hankalin Abdulbaseet yaji yana son sanin mai ke faruwa duk da ba shiga sabgar mutane yake yi ba balle ayi dashi. 
Sai yanzu ya kallli face dinta dan ko dazu daya tare mata kaya bai kalli fuskarta ba, kallo daya yayi mata ya gane halin da take ciki, kawai ji yayi kafafunsa sun dauke shi sun kaishi inda take ba tare da amincewar sa ba hannun sa yasa a aljihu ya zaro ATM CARD ya bawa cashier din bakinsa kuma ya furta cire kudin ka cikin dashashi yar muryarshi sai kace wanda ya shekara bai yi magana ba nan ma cikin harshen turanci, shi a zaton sa duka ba niyya yayi ba kafarsa da hannun sa ne sukayi🤣
Kallo ta bishi da shi kawai dan bata ma san mai zata ce ba, yana tsaye gefen ta sai satar kallon sa take yi shi kuwa bai ma san tana yiba, bakinta kuwa yayai nauyi kamar an dinke shi ta kasa furta komai bare kalmar godiya.
suna tsaye har cashier din ya gama ya miko masa card dinsa ya karba ya zura a aljihu batare da ya dako abun da yazo siya ba ya fito. 
Ko da tafito tunanin yanda zatayi ta koma gida take tayi tana tafiya tana tsara yarda zatayi har ta yanke shawarar zata tsare dan sahu idan yaso idan taje gida ta shiga ta dako masa kudin.....
Horn din mota taji akan ta da har ya gigitata tayi saurin tsayawa, motar da ta kade ta tabass ita ce wannan hummer baka tabasss ita ce wannan, gaban ta ya soma bugawa tara tara cak ta tsaya me zatayi gudu ko neman temako  da dabure take dan harga allah a tsorace take , full light 💡 din da ya sakar mata ya kara razana ta, ido ta rufe tana shirin yin ihun neman temako 🙆🏽‍♀ taji yace 
"Do you have money for taxi?" "I thought u lost ur card and money" jin murya ta daban yasa tayi saurin bude ido dan ganin wanda ke magana, ga mamakin ta shi din dai ta gani ya zuro kansa ta window yana kallon ta 👀 
  Tooooohhhhh inji mai tallar totuwa 
Nan bugun zuciyarta ya karu zuwa goma goma  da sauri ta girgiza masa kai, tama rasa mai zata ce masa, ashe ba bahaushe bane ta fada cikin ranta, sai kuma chan tace i....... have cikin rawar baki kana kallonta kasan a tsorace take dashi..... bai jira jin mai zata ce ba ya figi motarsa yayi gaba ya barta a wajen a tsaye ta bi bayan motar da kallo cin mamaki
Ohhh ni salama yau naga ikon allah ta fada a filli.......................
Wannan shine karo na farko da Abdulbaseet ya taba yiwa wata ya mace magana tun da ya dawo Nigeria 🇳🇬 bai taba sha'awar kulawa ba ma balantana a yana shi, wani ikon allah kuma shi kanshi sai da ya bawa kansa mamakin kulata da yayi dan mata basa burge shi ko kadan hasalima gani yake duk mata kazamai ne. Tun da allah ya hallici Abdulbaseet bai taba kulla wata ya mace a matsayin budurwa ba ko kawa ba, balantana wata lalata da alfasha tazo kansa ba, babu wanda ya yarda da shi a duniya daga iyayen sa har abokansa duk da ba ma abokai ne da shi ba , mutun daya a duniya Abdulbaseet yake sakin jiki yayi wa magana ta tsawon minti biyar wato kanwar sa Hajar dan ko naima ma ba shiri suke yi ba. Shi a ganin sa magana ma laifi ce shi yasa ya kwanmace kada yayi idan ma yayi toh haka zata fito a dakushe alamun ba niya, idan kuma yaga dama sai kaji ya saki baki yayi magana irinta mutane ya saki zazakar muryar sa.
Abu daya zamu rika mu yabi Abdulbaseet da shi shine taimako, duk inda yaga mutun na bukatar temako musan man idan dai na kasa da shine to Abdulbaseet bazai yi kasa a gwiwa ba dan gannin ya temaka maka, ko da niya kuwa ko ba niya kuwa sai ya temaka dan bazai iya gani ya share ba.
****************************************************
To masu karatu a nan zamu tsaya wannan satin 
Ku biyo mu sati mai zuwa domin jin yada zata kaya tsakanin Abdulbaseet da kuma salama

[11/16, 8:46 AM] Aysha Ado Albasu: Episode5⃣
              ‼Abdulbaseet ‼
Na:Bintnagz (Hasna mansur nagoda)
Checkbout on wattpad @bintnags

Engineer bature ahmad shine mahaifin su Abdulbaseet kwararen engineer ne mai ji da naira dan naira har kuka take a ajihun Engineer bature, yan asalin garin kano ne kuma suna zaune a sharada phase 3,matarsa ta farko hajiya Aisha ita ce mahaifiyar Abdulbaseet wannda wajen hai huwarsa allah yayi mata rasuwa, ba'a jima da rasuwar hajiya Aisha ba kuwa Engineer ya kara aure dan gani yake yana bukatar wace zata kular masa da Abdulbaseet. Abdulbaseet yana da wata bakwai a duniya Engineer ya auro hadiza, Hadiza a lokacin tana yarbudurwa.... koda Hadiza ta shigo gidan kuwa sai Engineer ya bude mata ido da naira, ya zamanto bata komai sai dan naira, a haka dai har ta shekara 5 bata haihu ba hakan yasa ta bazama wajen likitoci da malamai domin neman haihuwa, ama shiru dalilin haka kuwa ta dora wa kanta tsanar Abdulbaseet dan gani take idan bata haihu ba shikenan tata ta kare komai da Engineer ya malaka zai tafi hannun Abdulbaseet ne, hakan kuma bazai mata daidai ba, hakan yasa ta bazama dan neman yanda zata halaka shi tun kafin ya girma ya san mai duniya take ciki ya zame mata alaqaqai, gashi ya tashi da son mahaifin sa haka zalika shima mahaifin na mutukar son sa.
Duk yanda taje ta zagayo aikin ta baya taba cin ma burin ta haka take ta asarar kudi kusufa kusufa lungu lungu....hakan kuwa bai sa ta hakura ba har sai da ta raba kan Abdulbaseet da Engineer , ya tsane shi baya san kasancewa tare da shi koda na minti daya ne idan ma yayi to duka ne da zagi zasu biyo baya haka yayi ta shan wannan azabar har sai da yakai shekara biyar a duniya. Nan Engineer bature yace ai wllh karatu zai tura shi kasar waje duk dan ya batar da shi daga ganin sa, duk da kananan shekaraun sa kuwa hakan bai sa ya yi tunani ya kara duba lamarin ba.
Dadi kashe Hadiza dama haka take so, duk da mutane da dama basu amince da hakan ba har mahaifiyar engineer bata amince da hakan ba ama da yake halima ta juya shi haka ya dage ya cije sai ya tura Abdulbaseet kasar waje,  
ai kuwa ba jimawa ya tura shi Kasar london  ba uwa ba uba ba yanuwa ba dagin iya bare na baba ga karancin shekaru , ai kuwa a haka aka yo masa visa aka sama masa makaranta da komai aka danka masa komai a hannun sa harta kudin kashewarsa a hannun dan shekara 5 aka danka masa suka sami wani agent suka hada shi da shi suka tafi tare.
Ita dai halima a ganin ta ta rabuda alaqaqai yaje chan ya karata.
Bai dade da tafiya ba kuwa allah ya azurta halima da ciki, cikin ikon allah ta haihu a wata tara ta haifi santa leliyar yarta Wada taci sunnan mahaifiyar Hadiza ake kiranta da Na'ima nan duniya tayiwa halima fari tass shagali da buduri babu wanda ba'ayi ba wajen sunnan naima ba, Engineer kuwa kamar an masa albushir da gidan aljannan nan yayi ta kyauta da motocin sa da filayen sa duk dan murna. Bayan shekara uku kuma Halima ta kara haifo wata yar budurwar itama taci sunnan mahaifityar Enj wato Hajara suke ce mata Hajar.
*******************************************^*********
Abdulbaseet kuwa tun yasa kafarsa kasar engila Allah ya hada shi da Mr drake glassman a airport ganin yana ta gararamba shi kadai duk wanda Abdulbaseet ya tambaya sai kaga ana ta share shi ba'a kulla shi, dayake yaro ne ga karancin shekaru tun da agents dinshi suka gama yi masa screening da komai da komai a airport suka rasa yanda zasuyi da shi suma, nan suka danka wa wani daga cikin ma'aikantan airport din suka ce ya  tura shi Nigerian embassy na nan kasar engila, toh dai haka yaro yayi ta gararan ba a airport su wanda aka hada dashi basu kaishi ba basu damu da shi ba basu yi masa komai ba dai, shi kuma daga nan ya bata sukayi ta neman sa suka rasa  , su kuma dan kar a chaje su da batan nasa hakan yasa sukayi gum da bakin su, cikin ikon allah nan mr drake glassman ya karaso gare shi ya tambaye shi ida zaije daga ina kuma yake, Abdulbaseet ya zaro takardun makarantar sa da aka sama masa admission ta danka masa, yace masa makaranta aka turo shi kuma bashi da kowa bai kuma san kowa ba, hakan yasa mr glassman ya tausaya masa ya dauke shi ya tafi da shi gidan sa.
mr drake glassman gwaro ne bashi da mata hakan yasa daga shi sai Abdulbaseet suke zaune a gidan, cikin sati biyu yayi wa Abdulbaseet registration ya siya masa littatafai da duk wani abu da Abdulbaseet zai bukata a makaranta ya kaishi.... shi yake kaishi shi yake sako shi.... Mr glassman ya zama tamkar mahaifinsa zaka rantse shi ya haife shi saidai ban bancin kama da basa kama kwata kwata 😉
A haka har Abdulbaseet ya girma ya shiga high school, mr glassman bookshop ne da shi, babu irin nau'in littafin da babu a shagon mr glassman, idan Abdulbaseet ya dawo daga makaranta nan yake tsayawa idan dare yayi kuma shi ya tafi gidan baban sa yazo har sai dare yayi sosai wajen 12 haka toh sai ya koma gida, haka rayuwa su tayi ta tafiya cikin kwanciyar hankali.
               *******************
Kwanci tashi yau shekara Abdulbaseet 17 a engila babu waya babu kudi babu komai daga wajen magaifinsa daga Nigeria dan shi Abdulbaseet ma ya manta dasu shi yanzu bashi da wani uba da ya fiye masa mr glassman dan shi yanzu  ma mutane da yawa Mike glassman suke ce masa, musanman yan makarantar su da sauran mutanen gari.
Tun da allah ya hada mr glassman da Abdulbaseet ya tambaye shi sunnan shi yace Abdulbaseet mr glassman yaji bazai iya ba hakan yasa yake kiran sa da Mike.

Ya girma ya zama kyakyawan namiji cikaken namiji, jikinsa kuwa burin ko wace ya mace ne,  ga nutsuwa da basira,ga hazaka da kyautata wa mutane ,batun al'adun mu kuwa da addini babu su a wajen Abdulbaseet saboda  bai taso yaga anayi ba, yahudanci da rashin addini su ya taso da su dan mr glassman ba shida addini.
  Cikin kayan maye kuwa babu wanda Abdulbaseet baya sha, da yake mr glassman cikaken dan giya ne, haka zasu sa kwalaben giya a gabansu dozen dozen suyi ta sha, hakan shine jin dadin su, dan kuwa Abdulbaseet tun yana shekara goma ya fara shan giya,
neman mata kuwa da sauran fasiqanci irin na turawa samm abdulbaseet baya sha'awar su ko kadan dan mata basa burge shi kwata kwata shi a ganin su duk kazamai ne. Kuma bai tashi yaga mr glassman yana yiba shima. 

Kwatsam wata ranar alhamis mr glass man ya tashi da wani ciwon ciki mai tsanani nan da nan Abdulbaseet ya ruga da shi asibiti ama kan a kai ga duba mr glassman rai har yayi halinsa😓 da yake ya dade yana ciwon a tsaitsaye har likitoci suke ce masa sai ya rage shan giyar sa, ama ina bazai iya ba, sai da ta kashe shi.
Toh fah nan duniya ta dawo wa Abdulbaseet sabuwa fill bashi da kowa yanzu a duniya baisan dangin sa ba bai san gidan suba, gashi gatan sa ya kare, ama a kulun alkawarin mr glassman shine zai kaishi gidan su.
Daf da mutuwar mr glassman, mr glassman ya bashi wata takarda yace masa kar ya bude sai randa babu shi a duniya, bayan rasuwar kuwa da sati biyu Abdulbaseet ya dako takardar ya bude ya soma karantawa...............

   Ko da ya bude wasikar , takardu uku ne aciki, hawaye kawai ke futa daga idin Abdulbaseet, nan yace ya malaka masa bookshop dinsa da dukiyarsa gabaki dayan ta ya cigaba da running din ta, dayar takardar kuma abun hannu ne wanda mr glassman ya nade masa shi a takarda, abun hannun ya dadde a hannun mr glassman shima daf da mutuwar sa ya cireshi da niyar zai saka wa dansa Abdulbaseet a hannu kasan cewar abun yayi shekara sama da 40 a gannun sa. Dayar takardar kuma adireshin gidan su Abdulbaseet ne na naija wanda mr glassman ya nemo masa koda wata rana yana bukatar komawa gida wajen danginsa.........
Tun daga ranar da Abdulbaseet ya makala abun hannun nan bai sake forewarn ba har kawo iyanzu.
Shekara daya sa mutuwar mr glassman  Abdulbaseet ya kamala makarantar sa harda masters dinsa dayake duk a tare yayi su,
Ya kamala karatun ne bangaren library and information science. Hakan yasa ya maida book shop din mr glassman kamar wani library, ga karatu ana yi ga kuma littatafai ana siyarwa abun gwanin ban sha'awa.
**************************************************
Chan gida kuwa gwaggo mahaifiyar Enj bature ita ta tada rigima ita wllh kwana biyu muna nan mafarki take yi dan gane da jikan ta Abdulbaseet dan haka wllh duk inda yake a duniya sai an nemo shi an kawo mata shi gabanta idan su basa san shi to ita tana son kayanta, yaro daga tafiya karatu kun banzatar dashi kanar ba dan cikin ku ba sama da shekara 20 tohhh wllh ko a dawo mata dashi ko ta bishi sai dai su zabi daya. 
Bakin ciki kuwa kamar ya kashe Hadiza, ya ta yarda kwalan mangwaro ta rabi da kuda ake son kara nano mata wani toh wllh sam bazata sabu ba bindiga a ruwa, nan ta shiga bin malamai dan a batar da shi a duniya hakan yasa akai ta neman Abdulbaseet a kasar engila bashi ba labarin sa har aka kuma kwasar wasu shekara biyun, inda rigimar gwago ta dada tuburewa dan ita wallahi engila zata tafi neman jikanta tunda su sun kasa, waya sani ma ko yana da rai ko bashi da rai🤷🏽‍♀
Cikin rokon allahn da gwago keyi yasa aka samo labarin Abdulbaseet, gwaggo da kanta ta keta hazo dan zuwa tahowa da Abdulbaseet, duk da shekarun da aka diba gwaggo na gannin Abdulbaseet ta ga jinin ta ajikinsa ta gane shi,shi kuwa gogan naku bai gane taba, ama hakan bai hana ya karbe su hanu bibiyu ba.
Sai da yaji batun su da abun da ke tafe da su daya ke shi da daya daga cikin abokan mr glassman  suka tarbe su, nan gwaggo tayi ta basu hakuri tayi tayi tayi harda kukan ta tana dan allah yazo su koma gida tare, ama qememe ya ki yace shi ba inda zaije.
Sai a yanzu da Abdulbaseet ya girma yake ta gane abubuwan da suka fari dashi, dadi da kari ma bazai iya tafiya yabar dukiyar sa ba anan.
Ganin Abdulbaseet ya dage hakan yasa abokin mr glassman yace su je sa dawo gobe a barshi ya kara tuna ni akai.
Koda suka tafi mr david ya zauna da Abdulbaseet yayi masa yan nasihohin su na yahudawa hakan yasa Abdulbaseet yace he will think about it.
Basu dawo ba sai bayan kwana biyu, a lokacin Abdulbaseet ya dan yi cooling down nan ya kawo wa gwaggo  hujar bazai iya tafiya yabar dukiyar mr glassman ba kasancewar babu mau juya ta sai shi...... nan gwaggo tayi masa alkawarin insha allahu zai dawo, ya dai je gida ko da shekaran daya ce yayi ya dawo, dimin ya samu yaga gida yaga dangin sa yayi aure,,,,, jin an ambaci aure yasa ya kara tuburewa shi bazai iyi aure ba kuma ba inda zaije. Da kyar aka shawo kan Abdulbaseet gwaggo ta sako shi a gaba sai 
gida Nigeria 🇳🇬 ***************************************************

[11/16, 8:46 AM] Aysha Ado Albasu: Episode 7⃣
            ‼Abdulbaseet‼
Na: Bintnagz (Hasna mansur nagoda)
Wattpad : Bintnags

Kafin gwaggo kuwa ta dawo hajiya hadiza ta gama shirye shiryen da zatayi akan Abdulbaseet da mahaifin sa da kuma gwaggo ita kanta. Yaran ta su kansu hajar da na'ima ta gama hure musu kunne dangane da Abdulbaseet na kada su sake su dauke shi a matsayin dan uwa domin kuwa Abdulbaseet makiyin su ne kuma rival din su ne.

Da yake kafin gwaggo ta tafi ta sa an gyara mata daya daga cikin apartment din kusa da nata, an kuwa gyara shi an kawata shi tamkar turai dayake dalla na aiki 💵 
A deren alhamis Abdulbaseet da gwaggo suka sauka, direba aka sa ya je ta dako su daga airport ya kawo su gida cikin dare, babu wanda ya tarbe su kuwa,dan duk sun kwanta bacci, gwaggo dai batace komai ba tasa yan aiki suka kai masa kayan sa apartment dinsa, shi kuma tace yaje yayi wanka ya chanja kaya ya fito su ci abinci sai yakoma yaje ya huta.
 Washe gari Abdulbaseet bai tashi ba sai 9am gwaggo tasa akayi masa breakfast na gani na fada, ta kira kuma sauran yan uwan nasa da mahaifiyar su apartment dinta, dan kuwa Engineer tun karfe 7am ya fita wai ya tafi aiki.
Da salamar su suka shigo,  cikin nutsuwa suka ja kujerun dinning table suka gaishe da gwaggo, kallo kawai yabi su da shi dan gani yake tamkar yazo wata duniyar dan mutanen sun banbanta da na kasar su .Yanda basu sakar masa fuska ba shima bai sakar musu ba ya daure abun sa.
Sai da suka zauna suka fara cin abinci sannan gwaggo tace Hadiza ga danki nan yanda zaki riki yayan ki amana haka na baki amanar abdulbaseet kaima Abdulbaseet ga Hadiza nan ka dauke ta tamkar uwa a gare ka kayi mata biyaya kaji Abdulbaseet, duk da Abdulbaseet ba gane mai suke cewa yake yi na ama shiru yayi bai tanka musu ba, in banda girgiza kai da yake yi.
Hajar ce tace gwaggo na zata ai yayan namu baya jin hausa.....gwaggo ta danyi murmushi tace ina kyautata zaton haka dan tun jiya banji yace komai ba sai dai gyada kai da ina kwana, dariya suka yi baki daya, naima tace yaya do you get what we are saying? Murmushi yayi yace Dont worry about me sister , am a fast leaner but the language sounds different.
Sukayi dariya baki daya suka ci gaba da cin abincin su, ama hadiza dai fuskar nan a daure take.
Sai da akayi kwana biyu sannan Abdulbaseet yasa mahaifinsa a idon sa, yauma kasan cewar lahadi babu inda zaije gwaggo ta kira shi bangaren ta shi da Abdulbaseet, sai fusge fusge Engineer yakeyi da shan kunu kamar yaga kashi, dan koda ya gaishe shi bai amsa ba,
Nan dai gwago tayi tayi masa nasiha da fada sannan tace Abdulbaseet ya basu guri
Nan gwaggo tace da Enj wallahi talahi idan har ya nunawa Abdulbaseet banbancin a tsakanin shi da yaran Hadiza wllh bata yafe masa ba. Babu yanda ya iya haka yace toh sannan ya tashiya tafi.

Haka rayuwa tayi ta tafiya a gidan Engineer babu ruwan kowa da kowa a gidan kowa sabgar gabansa kawai yake yi babu mai kula shi sai gwaggo sai kuma yar kanwar sa hajar da takan zo dakin sa su danyi hira shima sai ta faki idon maman su batanan sannan, naima kuwa ko hanya suka hada ita da Abdulbaseet sai ta chaza ta,mahaifiyar ta ma haka dayake ita halin su daya da mahaifiyar ta, abinci kuwa wannan sai sai gwaggo ta bashi, Engineer kuwa sai ma suyi sati basu hadu ba, babu ruwan sa da shi. 
Gwaggo tace ya sama wa Abdulbaseet aiki a company din su Engineer ama keme me Engineer yaki yace ai basu da vacancy ama zai dunga bashi albashi duk wata, kasan cewar abun Hadiza na aiki akan gwaggo ita ma hakan yasa ta yarda ba tare da ta musa ba, hakan yasa duk wata Engineer ke bawa Abdulbaseet dubu dari biyar.
Toh tun gwaggo tana iya bakin kokarin ta akan Abdulbaseet to ita ma yanzu abu yaci tura ita ma an mata dan sudabaru akansa, itama yanxu babu ruwanta da shi sai gaisuwa, abinci kuwa saidai ta sa a kaimasa apartment dinsha shi ma kuma yace kada a kara kai masa abinci apartment zai dunga neman abincin sa da kansa.
Haka rayuwa ta ke guna a gidan Engineer bature da ahalinsa............
Wannan shine baban dalilin da ya maida Abdulbaseet haka ya zama soo hopeless and depressed bashi da kowa wanda yake supporting dinsa wanda zai saurare shi ko kuma wanda zai tausaya masa kowa ya juya masa baya, bashi da kowa a nigeria bai san kowa ba gashi da wuyar sabo, dadi da kari baya san mutane, hakan yasa ya yanke shawarar komawa London,koda yayi wa gwaggo zancen komawa London cewa tayi kar ta sake jin ya ambaci London din nan, kada kuma ya sake shigo mata apartment idan har zancen tafiya zai mata, meye ba'a yi masa anan din idan zai tafi sai ta tatara ya koma ai, bala'i dai kala kala.
Haka yayi ta hakuri yana jiran opportunity din da zai koma tazo masa.
*****************************************************
      Abdulbaseet saurayi ne dan kimanin shekara 29 dogo ne na gske gashi fari tass shima ga dogon hanci, gashi fafada burin ko wace ya mace. Yana da wani irin hali na rashin son hurda da hada sabga da mutane, musaman mata, shi a tuna nin sa duk mata hankalin su a kwari yake, raini baban abun da ya tsana kenan shi yasa nesa nesa dai kawai. Ga rashin son magana dan shi sai ya kwana ya tashi baice ufan ba sai dai idan Hajar ta shigo dakin sa ta sashi magana dayake tasu razo daya, duk wani abu da zai sa Abdulbaseet ya zama normal human being ya kaurace masa, bama zai bi hanyar ba,idan kowa yayi dama to insha allahu idan Abdulbaseet yazo hagu zai yi, baya son a ga fiskar sa ko kadan hakan yasa idan har zai fita sai ya saka bakaken kaya, bai yarda da kowa ba bai kuma amince da kowa ba hakan yasa yake tafiyar da rayuwar sa shi shi kadai.....................

Nan ta yi ta jiran dawo warsa har wajen magariba ama shiru bai dawo ba, ta dauki waya ta kira shi, ama wayar sa akashe, haka dai ta hakura bata sanar da kowa ba har taga dare yana tayi ama yayanta bai dawo ba, toh wa zata fada wa ma, ama kafin ta yanke wani abu bara ta kara jira ta gani dai.

Chan kuwa asibiti gurin Abdulbaseet, masu jinya sunyi jungun jungun kamar wanda akayiwa mutuwa, can salama da sabeeha suka ce zasu wuce makaranta nan suka yi masa sallama suka fita suka barshi shi da yaya Ado.......
Yaya ado kuwa ba zaman komai  yake ba sai jin labarin Abdulbaseet......
Yanzu da gske kake nima in tashi in tafi in barka kai kadai anan..?
Ba damuwa wllh nagode kwarai da gske wlh nagode....
Ama dan allah ina so kafin ka tafi ka sama mun hotel din da zan zauna idan an salame ni da sunan ka ko kuma wani suna dai da ba nawa ba.....cikin harshen turanci 
Cikin bacin rai Ado ya juyo ya kale shi
Wai mai ke damun ka ne, ka fada mun matsalar da take tatare da kai, mai yasa ka ke nisanta kanka da dangin ka da iyayen kane, mun dako ka cikin maye mun kawo ka asibiti, atleast ko ba komai ya kamata ka sanar da mu halin da kake ciki, idan kuma baza fada ba toh wlh am not ready to help you, tashi yayi a fusace ya fice.
Dayake yaya ado yana hada masa da turanci da kuma hausa hakan yasa ya gane abun da yake fadi, yayi ajiyar zuciya ya gyara kwanciyar sa ya kwanta.
                      ******
Su kuwa su salama tun da suka fita sabeeha take mata zancen sa na ya hadu yayi wace tsiya yayi wace tsiya duk tabi ta rikice tana santin kyaunsa... ita kuwa salama kasa cewa komai tayi jikin ta duk yayi sanyi kamar ita ce bata da lafiyar, har sun shiga makaranta salama taji bazata iya zaman makarantar ba,kawai tasa kai ta fice ta koma asibitin.
Jin motsin shigo war mutun yayi saurin dagowa hada ido sukayi da tayi saurin sunkuyar da kai... 
SALAMA ya fada cikin mamakin ganin ta....
Duk kurin salama, ashe na banza ne ko daga ido ta kalle shi kasa yi tayi, ji tayi kamar ta koma dan a bakin kofa ta tsaya.
come in please......
Shiru bata ce komai ba bata kuma matsa daga inda take ba....
Shima shiru yayi ya bita da kallo kawai,
Do you want me to come and pick u up
Ganin yana kokarin tashi daga kan gadon da kyar yasa tayi saurin karasowa inda yake....
Garin kokarin tashi ashe ya tuje abun karin ruwan nasa jini sai diga yake....ganin jinin yasa tayi saurin cewa jini 😳 hannun sa ta kamo a rikice ta rike tana dane masa wajen zubar jinin.
Sun fi sakan 30 a haka ganin smiling din da yayi yasa ta farga da abun da tayi,tayi saurin sakin hannun tare da fadin bari naje na kira likita.... dayan hannunta ya kamo tare da girgiza mata kai alamar ba sai taje ba. Tsayawa tayi tana kallon shi sai kuma yace baki iya first Aid ba ai an koya miki ma makaranta ko??
Ta gyada masa kai a hankali, sannan yace to apply your skills 
Dayan hannun sa yasa ya miko mata flaster da kuma tissue da cotton ya bata.
Ba bu ko gargada ta cire masa allurar daga cikin jikinsa sannan ta sa cotton da flaster ta mane wajen...shi kuwa ba abun da yake yi sai faman kallonta sai kara burge shi take yi....
Guri ya nuna mata kan gadon gefen kafarsa data zauna, ita kuma ta kasa zama sai kujera ya janyo ta zauna tana kallonsa, tana wasa da yan yatsun ta.
 Da kyar ta samu bakin ta ta furta
Meyasa da kaji baka da lafiya kazo gidan mu mai makon ka tafi asibiti??
Murmushi ya dan gyara fuska sannan yace..... because you are all i have left.............

[11/24, 9:54 AM] Dr H M Nagoda: Episode 1⃣8⃣
              ‼Abdulbaseet‼
Na: Bintnagz( Hasna mansur nagoda)
Make sure to check out @bintnags on wattpad*************************************

Karfe 12 dare ama shiru  babu salama babu labarin ta, sunturi kawai take yi a tsakar gida dan tunda yaya Ado ya fita nemanta shima bai dawo ba har yanzu, zikiri kawai take tana salati, tun da ta fita makaranta karfe bakwai har yanzu bata dawo ba, sun kira wayar ta kuwa shiru bata shiga. 
Sun kira sabeeha ita kuma tace ai a asibiti ta barta gun Abdulbaseet, itama tun dazu take kiranta wayar ta bata shiga.... nan hakalin bodado ya kuma tashi wannan wane irin iftila'i keneman afka musu.
Kai tsaye yaya ado yana fita asibiti yayi, ya tambayi mutanen reception suka ce ai tun da rana suka salami Abdulbaseet, shi da kanshi yayi clearance ya fita tare da salama....
Yaya ado yayi wani huci tare da jan wani mugun tsaki..... toh shi yanxu ina ne gidan su Abdulbaseet???
Mashin dinsa ya hau ya koma gida dan kuwa ya kamata ummansu ta san da wannan zance...
Koda ya koma gida ya tarar da hankalin bodado a tashe, ganin sa yasa ta tunkaro shi da tambayoyi kala kala
Nan ya kwantar mata da gankali ya ce mata akwai maganar da yake sk ya fada mata ama sai ta kwantar da hankalin ta.
Nan ta samu ta dan nutsu sannan ya samu ya zayano mata labarin Abdulbaseet yanda suka hadu da shi da kuma kyautata zaton sa na salama na hannun sa.
Inallilahi wa ina ilaihi raji'un kawai take maimaitawa wasu zafafan hawaye kawai ke zuba daga idon bodado mayafi ta zura wai ita a wannan daren sai taje gidan su Abdulbaseet,
Yaya ado dai yayi ta bata hakuri yana tayi wa allah ta bari sai gari ya waye, nan kuma fadan ya koma kan yaya ado akan mai zai munafirce ya ya boye mata abun, tana masa kallon mai wayo ashe bashi da wayo da zai jefa kanwarsa cikin wannan hali, toh zancen ma shine akan mai Abdulbaseet zai dauki salama, menene dalilin sa na yin hakan, haka dai tayi ta mita tana neman temako wajen allah.
*************************************************
Chan kuwa gidan su Abdulbaseet tun washe garin da Hajar ta ji shiru shiru bata ji shi ba har safiya, gari na waye wa ta je apartment dinshi, ta duba dakin sa babu alamar ya shigo balle ya fita , ta kara kiran number din shi ama shiru har yanzu ba'a kunna ba , tsaki taja tare da fadin wai yaya meye haka ne ?? Were in this world are u ???
Har zata fita murfin drawer dinsa ya riko ta, hakan yasa ta koma zata cire rigarta daga jiki, mai zata gani drawer Abdulbaseet wayam babu komai, a'ah ya haka ?? Ta fada a fili..... 
nan hankalinta ya tashi ta hau bubude masa wall-drops fa mamakin ta babu komai a ciki inda yake ajiye akwatinan sa ma babu inda yake ajiye wines din sa ma babu komai sai kwalabe ya kwashe sauran......
Wani ihu ta saka tare da dafe kanta a guje ta fita, kai tsaye apartment din gwaggo tayi......
Gwaggo na kan dining dinta tana shan coffee tana duba mujalar hausa ta jiyo ihun Hajar....
Hajar wai yaushe zakiyi wayo ne fisabilillahi mai ke damun ki ne zaki shigo mun da ihu da safiyar fari....
Gwaggo yaya..... yaya
Yaya me?
Gwaggo yaya ya gudu ya kwashe kayan sa gaba daya......
Wane yayan.....?? 
Abdulbaseet.......
Ya shiri gwaggo ta mike tayi bangaren Abdulbaseet cikin hanzari har tana tun-tube ..... wayam babu kowa babu komai, yanzu wannan yaron ni zai tona wa asiri, mu zai maryar kanana mutane gwaggo ta fada cikin bacin rai da takaici, ke maza je ki kira mun bature da hadiza maza nace......
A guje Hajar ta tafi tana kwala musu kira....
Ba shiri suna suka yayibo suka fito, nan suka iske labarin guduwar Abdulbaseet......

Engineer ne yayi ajiyar zuciya yace gwaggo kun san dalilin guduwar sa ko, yaron nan ku duba kuyi la'akari da halim sa da kuma dabiarsa babu yarda za'ayi ya amince da abin da kuke kokarin yi masa. Idan har abun ya kai ga haka dan allah gwaggo ku rabu da shi ya tatara ya koma inda ya fito, shima ya huta, ku kanku kun huta........

Rufe mun baki wallahi bai isa ya maida mu kananan mutane ya tona mana asiri a bainar jama'a ba, a wane dalili mun zauna mun tsara komai zai zo ya bata mana shiri, ko yana so ko baya so wallahi sai anyi, aure kamar ya auri rahama ya gama ne.....

Gwaggo ni naga ma temako ne muke yi masa, a gani na wace macen ce zata ce tana son bayahude, in ba ita rahaman da take son sa tsakani da allah ba inji hadiza

Cikin kuka Hajar tace mommy koma meye dan allah ayi idan mun nemo shi, its not the time to discuss this, bashi da wajen zuwa wallahi, bashi da kowa bai san kowa ba......
Saukar mari taji a fuskar ta, hajar ba nace miki ki dena shiga zancen manya ba, tafi ki ba mutane waje..... 
nan dai sukayi ta jainja Engineer ya gaji da jin wannan shirmen yayi shirme ya koma ciki.....

Shiru shiru yau kwana biyu basu ji duriyar Abdulbaseet ba, basu fita nema ba ba kuma su tabuka komai ba, su a ganin su suna zaune zai dawo gare su......

Sun gana breakfast kenan hadiza  ta dan yi  gyaran murya tace, yaron nan fa shiru har yanzu, ya kamata muyi wani abu akai, 
Ni abun ma mamaki yake bani wllh....kardai ace ya koma engila inji gwaggo
Bazai iya komawa ba gwaggo dan tun a ranar nace a rufe mana passport dinsa.....inji hadiza 
Engineer ya juyo ya galla mata wata muguwar harara alamun kinsan kuwa mai kike yi Hadiza...
Abu daya ya kamata kawai muyi yanzu idai har muna so ya dawo..... inji hadiza
Maiye shi..... inji gwaggo 
Kawai mu rufe bank account dinsa shine hanya mafi sauki da zai sa ya dawo cikin kankanin lokaci tunda ba inda zai samu.....

Engineer ne ya harzuka dan kwakwalwarsa ta fara dena daukan wannan lamari na gwaggo da hadiza.....gwaggo dan girman allah ku rabu da yaron nan dan darajar allah, wallahi hakan ba shi zai sa ya dawo ba, garin neman ya dawo zaku kara jefa shi cikin wani hali ne dan girman allah gwaggo..... hadiza kema ya ishe ki idan kika kara sa kanki a harkar Abdulbaseet wallahi ban yafe miki ba hadiza wallahi a bakin auren ki.... ya tashi a fusace ya fice, 
Yana kan fita kenan suka jiyo hayaniya a bakin gate.... 
Nan suka dunguma su duka zuwa bakin gate din..............


****************************************************

[11/24, 9:54 AM] Dr H M Nagoda: Episode 1⃣7⃣
              ‼Abdulbaseet‼
Na: Bintnagz( Hasna mansur nagoda)
Make sure to check out @bintnags on wattpad*************************************

Jin tayi masa tsawa bai sa ya sakar mata hannu ba dan shi bai ma kawo shi a matsayin lefi ba, jin ta fara kokawa da hannun ta yasa ya sakar mata hannu, 
What's wrong with you were are we going, why won't you explain things to me please, Hajar is even calling me and u dnt want to talk to her or let her knw wats wrong with u...
Agogon hannun sa kawai ya kalla bai ce mata komai ba ya tura ta cikin mota, ya shiga shima ya tada mota......
Let me go ..... i said let me go Abdulbaseet yaune karo na farko da ta lalubo sunan shi ta fada...let me go please what the hell is wrong with u........
Helpppppppp
Salama ya fada cikin sasanyar muryar sa, 
Am not a bad person salama, u have to trust me, its just a help,,,,,,,
Which kind of help is this had i knw I wouldn't have trust u in the beginning, how can this be a help, help u archive ur unwanted deeds or wat, what kind of human being are u......
Ganin zata tara masa mutane yasa kawai ya tada mota ya soma tafiya.
Salati kawai take yi, jikinta sai bari yake yi, neman hanyar kubuta kawai take daga sharin Abdulbaseet.
Hawaye kawai ke zuba daga idon ta, ganin hakan yasa hankalin sa ya kuma tashi shima dan tun da ta fara ihu hankalin sa ya tashi ama me bazai iya rabuwa da ita ba ita kadai ce farin cikinsa a duniya ita kadai ce wada zai iya dogaro ta ita.... to akan me zai kyale ta.
Guri ya samu yayi parking, 
Ya kira sunan ta shiru bata dago ba, hannun sa yakai zai dago fuskar ta jin saukar hannun sa yasa tayi saurin dagowa ta kwade hannun.
Murmushi kawai yayi ya kara kuna mota ya cigaba da tafiya.....
Bai tsaya ko ina ba sai banki, ko da zai fita kulleta yayi ya shiga ya gama abun da zai yi ya fito....ganin shi da makudan kudade ya shigo mota da su yasa hankalin ta ya kuma tashi....
Nan wani sabon kuka da ihu ya kuma barke wa.....
I beg u in the name of God Abdulbaseet let me go why are u doing this to your life saver , i saved u life, and why are u trying to kidnap me in the middle of a day, I never thought u were this bad, Hajar loves u with all her heart am sure ur parents do, dan allah dan annabi ka rufa mun asiri 
Shi kuma abunda take fadi dada harzuka shi yake yi take motar kawai yake yi kamar zai tashi sama. Inaaa hankalin sa kwata kwata baya jikin shi zuciyar ta fara tafar fasa.....
Abu da take fadi sosa masa rai yake yi ama mai bazai iya rabuwa da ita ba, baya son wani abu da zai iya raba shi da ita, gani yake idan  har ya rabu da ita toh tashi ta kare, bashi da wani a duniya da zai iya dogara da shi sai ita, ga kukan ta da ihun ta suna kara sosa masa rai wanda yake soya masa zuciya. Dadi da kari bai ma san inda zai je ba duk wannan gudun da yake falfalawa....
Har sun soma barin gari ga magaruba ta gabato gari ya soma yin duhu
Wani dan fili ya samu yayi parking motar sa,
Kifa kansa kawai yayi a steering motar wasu zafafan hawaye suka zubo masa, wani mugun ihu shima ya saki tare da buga kansa a jikin steering motar, sautin kukan sa taji yana fita a hankali, hakan yasa tayi saurin bude ido daga kukan da take yi ta kalle shi, 
Lalle da damuwa🤔
Bata ce masa komai ba ita ma ta fara jero nata sabon babin kukan,
Bude mota kawai yayi ya bude boot din mota ya dako kwalaben giya  guda biyu, sannan ya dawo, kan kice mai ya bude ya bulbule daya a ciki sa.....
Sai a lokacin ta kula da abun da yake yi ganin yana rikicin bude ta biyu.....
Subahanallahi ta fada tare da zaro ido.....
Abdulbaseet ta fada cikin kuka tare da kuma mama ki , dama ashe duk abun da nake raya wa kaina dede ne, wa'iya zibilahin, kaicho na temakon ka dana yi a rayuwa, nayi nadamar sanin ka Abdulbaseet....😭😭
Wayar ta ta tuno da ita ta fara lallube chan ta hango ta tayi saurin kai hannun ta ama ina har ya ruga ta .....
Budewa yayi ya cire sim card din ya cilar sannan ya chila mata wayar.....
Ya lalubo inda wajen mukullin motar yake ya kunna cikin maye ya cigaba da falfala gudu kamar zai tashi sama
In takaice muku labari har karfe tara na dare Abdulbaseet gudu yake yi a cikin motar nan ga salama tun tana kuka har ta dena jikin fa bari kawai yake yi kamar wadda ke cikin snow ta a yan adduoin ta idon ta a rufe kuwa gam ta takure kamar me a waje daya....
Bai san inda yake ba goggle map din motar sa yayi using ya ga inda yake, yanda ya daga ido zai kalla ita ma haka ta daga ido dayake yar danja ta kama alamun network ya dawo.........
KADUNA 😳 suka fada a tare shi da ita......

Motar ce ta kufce masa kasan cewar hankalinsa ba akan motar yake ba, motar ta ja shi sukayi gefen titi, motar tayar ta fashe kasan cewar ta gogi kwalta ta bakin titi mai kurji kurji, ta jashi tayi daji dashi, allah ya rufa asiri motar bata yi juyi da shi ba.
Kansa ne ya bugu da jiki sitiyarin motar, shiru kake ji cikin dajin nan babu kowa sai kukan tsuntsaye da gyare da tsaka babu abun da kake ji.....
Chan ya daure ya dago kansa, kansa wani matsanancin sarawa yake masa, ji yayi yana ganin abu bibiyu, da kyar yasa hannu a aljihu ya zaro wayar sa ya duba lokaci, karfe 9:20
Ama ya akai dare yayi duhu haka kamar 12 dare.....
ji yayi kansa kamar zai fado, kawai ya danna (pressing lock) lock din motar ya gyara zamam kujerar sa ya shigida ko yaji dadi dai.
Bawan allah da yake abubuwa sun masa yawa a cikin kai hakan yasa ya rasa tunanin mai ma zai yi ga kai na damun sa, kan kice mai har yayi bacci, ga mutumin naku da shegen nauyin bacci.
Dare yayi wajen p karfe biyu dare kenan, fulanin daji wanda ake kira da mugu da makamin, su ka fito patrol din su.
Titi suka fito suka tsaya sun filancin sun juyi da gora a hannun su, sun tare mota ta biyu kenan suna musu kwace, idan kuma kayi musu taurin kai sai su rade ka da gora su kwace har motar su barka a wajen, idan allah yayi ka tashi toh, idan kuma daga nan ka margaya dama faduwa ce tazo dadi da zama.
Daya daga cikin su ne ya gangara cikin dajin dan yin fitsari ya gama kenan ya dago yana zuge tazuge ya hango motar Abdulbaseet.....

Ni mahaifiyar ki ce tun da nake, tun da na taso a rayuwa ta ban taba gani ko naji wata ya mace ta bude bakin ta tace tana son da namiji ba a rayuwar mu ta fulani.
Bama haka ba salama kin kuwa san mai kike yi, yaron da bashida kirki bashida shedar arzuki ko awajen iyayen sa shi zaki rarumo mana....
To wallahi kulla, kada na sake jin kinyi maganar wannan yaron a gabana, ba ma gabana ba babu ke babu shi koda bayan raina, ki je chan ki nemo wani, ko kuma ki amince da murtala dan uwan ki da yake da sillah da asali mai kyau...
Tana kaiwa nan ta tashi ta shige daki....

Nabeel bai bar wajen ba sai da ya tabatar yayi clearing duk wani miss understanding din da ke zuciyar yaya ado dan gane da Abdulbaseet
Ama bai fada masa shi yayi kidnapping kanwarsa ba yadai fada masa yana da family issue ne da bazai iya zama da su ba shi yasa ya zabi ya bar musu garin kawai haka dai suka kare labarin nasu.....
Sai da daddare nabeel ya sami baban su yayi masa bayani mai kyau dangane da Abdulbaseet, yaji labarin kwarai da gaske dan kuwa ya taba shi sosai, ya kuma yi alkawarin insha allahu zai yi ma malam kabir daurawa magana idan yaso sai su ga yanda abin zai kasance
Ama me yana so ya fara ganin Abdulbaseet tukunna.....
Washe gari da sasafe suka bayana shi da Abdulbaseet suka tarar da shi a baban pallor dinshi....
Bayan sun gagaisa sannan babban nabeel yace da Abdulbaseet ya ji labarinshi daga gurin nabeel kuma yayi alkawarin zai taimaka masa kwarai da gske kuma yana murna da jin hakan, ama me Abdulbaseet sai yayi hakuri yakuma kai zuciyar sa nesa ya kuma iya zama da mutane....
Sai kuma yayi alkawarin zaiyi watsi da dukkin al'ada irin ta bayahude da nasara domim kuwa haramun ce a addinin musulimci,
Sai kuma yasa a ransa zai karni addini musulimci ba wai dan yarinyar da yake so ba, sai yayi alkawari ya kuma sa wa ransa ba dan ita ba dan ceto kansa daga halaka, ya kuma gano addinin gaskia...
Mahaifinshi ma sai yayi alkawarin zai koma musu ya nemi gafar shi ko ta halin yaya, sannan ya nuna musu baya son auren yarinyar da aka bashi idan kuma har sun dage da sai ya aure ta to ya karbi kadara domin kuwa mumini dole yayi imani da kada mai kyaunta ko mara kyaunta.
Addinin musulunci kuma ya halata masa aurem mata har 4 saboda haka idan ya auri wadda iyayen sa suka ummarce shi da ya aura yana da ikon karo guda uku idan har yana da halin kula da su idan kuma zai yi adalcin a tsakanin su.
Nan dai yayi tayi musu wa'azi da nasiha mai shiga jiki sannan yace su tashi su tafi zai neme su idan yayi wa mallam kabiru gombawa magana.....

Abdulbaseet addinin musulmi ya ginu bisa abubuwa guda biyar wato abun da ake kira da 5 pillars of islam in English
1.....is failth wat we call shadda in islam am going to recite and then you would repeat after me
Nan take aka bawa Abdulbaseet kalmar shahadda ya karba suka yi kabar baki daya suka kuma taya shi murna da karbar addinin musulimci
2.... is salah ...Salah consists of five daily prayers according to the Sunna; the names are according to the prayer times: Fajr (dawn), Dhuhr (noon), ʿAṣr (afternoon), Maghrib (evening), and ʿIshāʾ (night). The Fajr prayer is performed before sunrise, Dhuhr is performed in the midday after the sun has surpassed its highest point, Asr is the evening prayer before sunset, Maghrib is the evening prayer after sunset and Isha is the night prayer
3..... is zakkat The word zakāt can be defined as purification and growth because it allows an individual to achieve balance and encourages new growth. The principle of knowing that all things belong to God is essential to purification and growth. Zakāt is obligatory for all Muslims who are able to do so.
4.....is fasting. fasting is an obligatory act during the month of Ramadan.Muslims must abstain from food and drink from dawn to dusk during this month, and are to be especially mindful of other sins.Fasting is necessary for every Muslim that has reached puberty (unless he/she suffers from a medical condition which prevents him/her from doing so).
5.... pilgrimage to macca ( hajj)
The Hajj is a pilgrimage that occurs during the Islamic month of Dhu al-Hijjah to the holy city of Mecca. Every able-bodied Muslim is obliged to make the pilgrimage to Mecca at least once in their life.
Waddanan sune shika shikan musulinci mallan Abdulbaseet

Idan muka hada lokacin waje daya yau shekara daya kenan chir da kuluwar soyayyar Abdulbaseet da kuma abar kaunarsa salama
Cikin shekara daya kar kaso kaji yanda Abdulbaseet ya karu sossai da addinin musulunci, shi da kanshi yanzu idan yaga mutun yana abu zaice babu kyau haramun ne, kuma allah yayi hani ga hakan, karatu kuwa yanzu izun shi ashirin da biyar a Alqurani, ga kuma sauran littatafai da ya sauke, salah kuwa babu wada take wuce shi, ko mai yake yi ana kiran sallah zai mike ya je ya gaishe da ubangijin shi.
Lalle adinin allah da fadin allah gaskia ne, allah yayi mana hikima da baiwa kala kala ya kuma saukaka mana abubuwa da yawa ya kuma sa mana waraka ta hanyar bauta masa domin kuwa duk wannan bakin ran da jin kan shi da kuma rashin kaunar mutanen shi duk sun rage domin kuwa yanzu yana hurda da mutane sosai, duk da ba wani hurda ba kawai dai gaisawa da mutane, duk sanda zai je masalaci zai gaisa da mutane, yayin dawo warshi ma zai gaisa da mutane, hakan yasa yanzu mutane sun fara sanin shi da dama, domin kuwa yana girma ma mutane sosai.
Soyayya kuwa ba'a maganar ta domin kuwa duk da basa ganin junan su shi da salama hakan nai hana su yin waya ba a kowace rana, duk wani activities dinshi ta sani shima kuma ya san nata activities din domin kuwa har timetable dinta na makaranta ya sani.
Duk da rigimar da zata zo nan gaba idan zancen auren su ya bayana, hakan bai sa sunyi kasa a gwiwa ba dan ganin sun farantawa junan su ba, sun kuma dauki junan su da mahimmanci ba. Giving up baya cikin lissafin wadan nan masoyan saboda wen ever there is way toh tabass there is hope inji malan bature.

Shima farooq ya mike yana haba wallahi daddy biri yayi kama da mutun tun da na ganshi wallahi har yanzu ya kasa fita daga ido na dana ganshi anty halima make tunawa da ita, shi yasa ma nace yau sai na nuna maka wannan hoton dan tun da naje kaduna wajen farida last week na taho da hoton, ama ita farida bata gane ba.
Alhj sunusi ya jin jina kai ba shiri yace da farooq suje , farooq ya bishi a baya suka fita ya tuka shi sai gidan mahaifiyar su halimatu.
Dangin mahaifiyar Abdulbaseet 
Halimatu da kuma sunusi su biyu allah ya bawa iyensu, maifiyar su wadda suke kira da inna falmata sai kuma mahaifinsu mal indimi da allah yayi wa rasuwa tun halimatu da sunusi suna kanana. Sun taso cikin jin dadi da dukiya da kwanciyar hankali sai dai matsala guda da suka samu ita ce basa barin bare yazo wajen su aure basa bayar wa kuma basa karba.
Wannan shi ya hadasa matsala lokacin da mahaifin Abdulbaseet Engineer bature yazo neman auren halimatu, anyi rigima anyi rigima a tsakanin dangi biyi ama da yake soyyaya ta ruga ta yanka hakan yasa suka hakura suka bar halima ta auri wanda take so wato Engineer bature, da yake ba cikin ruwan sanyi aka yardar wa bikin ba hakan yasa basu yi wani shagali ba kawai aka daura aure aka kai halima garin kano.
Da yake halima bata kwaso tsarin rai da yawa ba hakan yasa wajen haihuwar danta na fari wato Abdulbaseet ta rasa ranta, hakan yazo musu da takaici da alhini ama ba yarda suka iya haka suka hakura suka barwa allah komai.
Sunyi sunyi su karbi Abdulbaseet tun yana jariri ama Engineer da gwaggo suka tubure suka ki da wannan zancen, nan ma kotu ta so ta rabasu ama hakan yasa basuyi nasara ba suka hakura suka bar musu, Abdulbaseet da yana yaro suna kawo shi duk hutu yazo yayi musu hutu ama tun da yakai shekara biyar a duniya basu sake ganin shi ba. Sai dai suka ji zancen karin auren Engineer da kuma tura shi kasar waje da sukayi run yana dan kankani, daga baya kuma suka riski labarin ai ya bata ba'a ma san inda yake ba.
Hakan ya koma musu rai ba kadan ba dan shi da kanshi alhaji sunu si sai da yaje neman Abdulbaseet ama bai sami labarin shi ba, 
Haka ya dawo suka fawala wa allah komai a hannun shi.

Inna falmata na kallon hoton Abdulbaseet ta fara hawaye kamar bazata tsaya ba ashe tana da rabon kara sa jikan ta Abdulbaseet a ido, ashe da rabon kara saduwa dan nan.

Inna falmata ta girgiza kai tace ina sam wannan jinnin ta ne ba makawa wannan dan gidan halimatu ne.
Nan alhaji sunusi yace toh bara su je idan yaso gobe da safe sai su dau hanyar zuwa kaduna,inna falmata ta tada rigimar ita ma sai taje, da kyar suka lalabata suka ce hanyar da nisa ga kuma jiki tana fama da shi ta zauna a gida ta huta yayi mata alkawarin zai kawo mata jikan ta har gida insha allahu.

Tun a lokacin ita ma faridah ta fara yan shirye shiryen tarar iyayen ta, ita da mijinta yaya ado dan cewa yayi shi ba inda zaije ma har sai sun zo.
Sai da la'asar suka karaso garin kaduna suna zuwa direct gidan faridah suka tsaya nan sukaci abinci suka huta sukayi salolim su sannan, faridah sai murna take yi yau ga maman ta da babanta a gidan ta ga kuma yayan ta abun gwanin ban sha'awa

Rayuwar wannan yaro ta salwanta, ta lalace, ya gaza kulawa, ya gaza samun so da kauna, kum bari kirsta shi ya reni danka, kirista shi ya ji tausayin dan ka ya raine shi kai baka ji qan naka dan ba, tun da nake a duniya bature ban taba ganin rashin imani ba irin wanda kayi wa dan nan.
Kasan da cewa danka bai cikin adinin musulimci ya taso ba, kasan da cewa danka christa ne, sai da ya dawo ya bar gida sannan ya gane cewa musulimci shine addinin gaskia.

Inna falmata ta juyo kan hadiza sannan tace allah hadiza jinji nace allah bazan yi kafara ba hadiza, kece ummul-abaisin din faruwar haka, hadiza yaya mata ne da ke, hadiza kiji taron allah, duniyar nan bakomai bace duniya ba matabata bace hadiza, yanda kikayi wa dan wani wallahi hadiza duniya sai tayiwa naki, ranin karya kurare ne hadiza, komi nisan jifa wallahi kau kasa zai yo.ki duba ki gani yau ina gwaggo ina halimatu, ina haifin ki, ina mahifinsu sanusi, ya duk suna ina, inda suka tafi duk chan zamu bisu wallahi.

Hadiza da tayi tsumu tsumu sai hawaye kawai take zubdawa, idon Engineer ya ciciko da hawaye, farooq da Abdulbaseet da suka chure waje daya suma share ido kawai suke yi...
Tari ya kama inna falmata alhaji sunusi yayi saurin mika mata kwalbar ruwa, yana mata sannu....
Alhaji sunusi ya ari bakin inna falmata yace, saboda haka abun da muka zo da shi shine
Engineer inaso ka dauki danka Abdulbaseet ka bani shi a matsayin da, ni kuma nayi alkawarin zan rike shi in aurar da shi, tamkar yanda zan rike yayana, ina so Abdulbaseet ya zama daya daga cikin yaya na kanar yanda mukaso tun farko hakan bai yiwu ba....

 Engineer ya sauke lumfashi ya share ido yace....
Hakikanin gaskia nasan nayi wa Abdulbaseet lefi, na cutar da rayuwar Abdulbaseet, nayi wasa da nauyin da allah ya dora mun a matsayina na uba...
Ya kalli Abdulbaseet da kansa na kasa ya kira sunnan sa, ya dago a sanyaye ya kalle shi...
Abdulbaseet ka dubi girman allah ka yafe mun Abdulbaseet, kamar yarda na sanar da kai Abdulbaseet nayi dana sanin dukan abin da ya faru gare ka, da ana dawo da baya dana koma domin wanke kaina a wajen ka, Abdulbaseet ka yafe mun a matsayina na mahaifin ka....
Ya kuma share ido yace babu komai daddy, dama ban taba tunanin kin yafe muku ba daddy...it's usual hakan zai iya faruwa ga kowa ba ni kadai ba its our fate and destiny, and am happy to be you son. 
Hadiza ma cikin kuka ta roki tata gafarar, ta kuma nuna da nasanin ta, da kuma rashin jin dadin abun da ta aikata shekarun baya da suka wuce, 
Shima kuwa Abdulbaseet baiyi kasa a gwiwa ba wajen gafarta mata ya kuma ji dadin hakan kwarai da gaske...
Engineer ya kalli Abdulbaseet yace ya amince da zance da kawun sa yazo da shi...
Cikin farinciki ya yarda kuma ya amince da hakan...
Engineer yace ya bar wa Alhaji sunusi Abdulbaseet halak malak duniya da lahira Abdulbaseet nashi ne, shima ya dauki alkawarin rike shi amana....
Nan suka kamala taro cikin farinciki da jindadi at last family ya hadu waje daya. Alhmdulillah🙏🏼
Nan suka kwana da dare yayi Abdulbaseet suka sami ganawa tsakanin uba da da, washe gari da safe bayan breakfast suka dauki hanyar garin maiduguri harda Abdulbaseet, bayan ya tatara  sauran kayan sa daga gidan Engineer, duk da yaso ya biya ta wajen salama ama bai sami lokacin ba.

****************************************************
Murna wajen sabon dangin Abdulbaseet na maiduguri kamar yau aka haifo shi, kowa so yake ya ganshi ya sa masa albarka, abun gwanin ban sha'awa da jin dadi, yau ga Abdulbaseet cikin dangi ga uwa ga uba ga farooq aboki kuma yaya duk da tsiran su bai fi shekara daya ba ga kuma kanne da ya tara, ga kuma kanwarsa farida kuma surukarsa.....kai abun dai sai godiya.
Satin sa guda a maiduguri ama ji yake yi duniyar ta chanja masa gaba daya ta juye ya zama tamkar wani sabon halita, alhaji Sunusi ya bashi daki kaya kudi har mota ya sake masa zuwa hadadiyar benz, babu abun da alhaji Sunusi bai yiwa Abdulbaseet ba cikin yan kwanakin nan, duk wani abu da zai bukata babu wanda bai samu ba, shikam yanzu babu abunda ya rage masa sai salama....

Yau suna zaune bayan sun gama dinner bintu last born dinsu mai kimanin shekara bakwai  tace, Yaya Abdulbaseet gobe kai zaka kaini makaranta, fine baby girl gobe zan kaiki makaranta sai ki sauri ki shirya, noor yayi saurin cewa sai kace ke kadai kike zuwa makaranta, Abdulbaseet yayi dariya yace fine noor kaima zan kaika makaranta yanzu ku tashi kuje kuyi homeworker ayi bacci, ni nayi nawa haka suka tashi suka shiga ciki....
Alhaji Sunusi ya mike yace Abdulbaseet ka same ni fallo, toh daddy ya fada...
Yana zaune kan kujera ya tarar da shi ya zauna cikin biyaya yace gani daddy...
Abdulbaseet zancen aiki fah, kana son yin aiki ne ko kanaso na baka daya daga cikin kantina na ka cigaba da running.
Ya danyi shiru sai cewa yayi daddy am sorry to say but I really appreciate all your efforts on me and all. Daddy for now i want to settle down maybe yanzu zan fara taya ka business dinka, kafin abubuwa su zama stable. Idan komai ya zama settle ina so zan koma England became all my dreams are there, i want to build my life over there, idan na zauna nan bazan yi amfani da karatuna ba yanda ta kamata, ina da library da book stores wajen guda uku a England, so ina so na yi manipulating dinsu in mai da books and other reading materials su zama source of income dina, i knw its going to be a great achievement.
Alhaji Sunusi ya jinjina kai alamar gamsuwa da dukanin abun da Abdulbaseet ya fada...
Ok son na gamsu da duk abinda ka fada and ni bazan hana ka yi abun da kake so ba, allah yayi maka abarka ya kuma albarkaci rayuwar my son, allah kuma ya tabatar maka da alheri a dukanin abun da kasa hannun ka akai.
Ama wen do you plan on leaving...
Ya danyi shiru sannan yace ina son in nemi na kaina tukuna, zan karbi daya daga cikin shagunan naka, nayi building up family, sannan na fara tunanin tafiya

Toh zancen aure kuma fa....!?

Samum guri irin na Abdulbaseet har dinner yau dauke wa yayi a gidan yaya ado dan cewa tayi restaurant zasu fita suci abinci, duk da yaya ado ba so yake ba ama ran faridah da salama datake kunyar sa na so, hakan yasa ya amince suka fita.
Restaurant din branch din marble hotel sukaje kowa yayi ordering abun da yake so, na gani na fada mai ciki da koshin lafiya. Da yake dama ance hanta da jin ba'a rabo mutanen namu na jone waje daya ga kujerar sa ga tata, abun da yayi ordering shi tayi ordering, ba halin yin magana da soyayya ga yaya ado ya kafa musu idanu, ga kuma faridah da kirisss take jira tayi magana dan bakin ta sai kaikayi yake, shi kuwa nabeel dan abi yarima a sha kida ne suna wuta yana binsu da petir😂
Hakan yasa suka sami nutsuwa sai kallo da suke wurga wa juna.
Haka ranar ta kare cikin raha da dariya, jin dadi anasuwa farin ciki babu wanda babu a tsakanin mutanen nan, Abdulbaseet da salama basu taba zata zasu riski wannan rana ba da kowa yake farin ciki da saukin taren su ba...
A daren ranar Abdulbaseet bai sami runtsawa wa sai da ya zayanawa salama duk kanin abubuwan da suka faru kwanakin baya da suka wuce, ya kuma bata hakurin rashin jin shi da tayi na lokacin mai tsawo haka, ama komai ya dadi daita yanzu babu wani abu da zai kara raba su....
Shima nabeel sai da yagama nashi nanaye sannan ya kwanta da yake gidan nabeel ya sauka............
***************************************************
BAYAN SHEKARA GUDA

Sai faman sunturi hadiza take yi a pallon ta waya a hannu ta kira ta kira ama ba'a dauka yanzu takai ga idan ta kira ma a kashe take jin wayar na'ima , nai'ma ko mutuwar gwaggo bata sani ba sai faman sun turi take yi taje ra dawo taje ta dawo ta rasa inda zata sa kanta, ta sanar wa da Engineer shima hankalin sa yadan daga na kwana biyu bayan nan kuma ya sakar mata akalar shirin. Ga tana ta kokarin samun visar zuwa engila ta nema ta rasa sun hana visa, ita kam ina zata sa kanta da abun kunyar da Na'ima  take shirin janyo mata...
Naima nachan ita da masoyin rayuwar ta lucus herry suna cin duniyar su da tsinke, kasar engila, makaranta kuwa dama ta dade da ajiye ta a gefe, dan tun da ta dawo hutu waccan shekar ta hadi da lucus komai ya tabarbare mata, ta zam whore din lucus, da da farko ya sota kamar hauka ama tunda ta bashi kanta ya gama amfani da ita ya yada ita, baya tashi kawo mata ziyara sai yana da bukata, shi kuma idan yazo har rawar jiki take wajen bashi kanta, yayi abin da zaiyi ta bata guri...
Sai kuma ya bushi iska, da farko bata ganin lefi n shi ita ma dan duk abin da yayi girma ne, to yanzu kuma taga bata samun shi yanda take so, idan ta ta bukatar ta zo mata shi kuma baya nan, hakan yasa ta fara bin mazan waje, duk wani club da ka sani hadadun babes da guys na zuwa to naima na wajen tana bin masa suyi amfani da ita a banza....
Suna kuwa ta dade da komawa namcy, dan namcy ya bita sama da kasa, dan har wani farin jini ne yanzu da ita wajen maza...
Cikin lokaci kalilan naima ta manta da allah da kuma fadin sa, shaye shaye, kwaya babu wada naima batayi, wa'iya zubillahi allah ka kiyashe mu da wannan mununar rayuwa, tabass idan har bawa yayi engaging kanshi cikin wannan badalar rayuwa to kai kasan babu allah a zuciyar sa domin kuwa idan za'a sawa naima wuka a wuya bazata ce maka ga ranar da tayi sallah ba.
Hakan yasa ta manta da gida, ta manta da mahaifiyar ta mai goya mata baya, a kowane hali ta shiga to hadiza sai ta sam yada ta zame naima daga ciki, naima ita ce ta hannun daman ta duk abin da hadiza zatayi koda kisa ne sai naima ta bi bayanta tace mommy ai wannan daidai ne, toh ga irin ta nan........
Dan kwata kwata halin su bai zo daya da hajar ba, dan har takan tsokane ta da ai hajar ba jinin mommy bace.....
Toh wannan kenan............

Chan kuwa farin maiduguri, harka ta bude mutumin naku sai fachaka yake cikin naira da kauna , domin kuwa Alhaji sanusi ya dauki dukiyarsa gaba dayan ta ya danka wa farooq da Abdulbaseet ya basu ragamar cigaba da gudanar da rayuwar gidan da shi kanshi, su ciyar da shi da iyalen sa da kuma su kansu da nasu iyalin dan farooq shima ta dadago zancen aure...
Hakan yasa Abdulbaseet ya sami abunyi sabga tayi masa yawa, dan hakan baya samun lokacin zuwa ganin abar kaunar sa. Saidai kullun cikin video call yake dan bau wanda basuyi call da shi ba cikin kannen shi....
Cikin watan karshe shekara da ta wuce  allah ya sauki farida, ta haifo yarinyarta yar kyakyawa mai kama da salama, wada ranar suna taci suna AISHA. Kai kar kaso kaga murma wajen yaya ado da Bodado da salama, da yake kan ta haihu ta koma gida, salama taso zuwa suna ama kasancewar suna exams bata zo ba, sai hotunan ta da kullun sai Abdulbaseet ya dauka ya tura mata, saboda tun da aka ce yarinyar nan na kama da salama Abdulbaseet ya kamu da kaunar yar jaririyae nan, har kirari farida take wa yar kaga yar Abdulbaseet mai kama da salama....
Saidai tayi dariya kawai ya sunkuyar da kai....
 Yau farida da yan kannenta na zaune a fallo bintu na kallon cartoon, noor kuma sai game yake bugawa, mummy aziza na gefe rike da news paper ita kuma farida na bawa baby nono, samarin gidan suka shigo yan biyun mommy aziza kenan Yaya Abdulbaseet da yaya farooq....
Farida ta fara kirarin nata yaya Abdulbaseet gafa yar taka, yar gidan yaya mai kama da salama 😂
Yayi dariya yace to ki kwantar da hankalin ki kema kinsan salama tafi yarinyar nan kyau, haba haba wallahi a'ah baka ganin hancin ta irin na umma Bodado ai ko salama bata kwaso kyaun umma Bodado ba wallahi...
Ta kali farooq da ke wa mommy aziza rada tace tace a'ah yaya dan gidan mommy na hamida bada kanka a sare...
Da dago yace ke zan mare ki wallahi, duk kin bi kin ishi mutane da surutu, bakin ki baya gajiya ne😏 ni banga mai ado ya gani a jikin ki ba wallahi, shi salihin bawa ba ruwan shi da magana ke kuwa surutu kamar kinci aku kuturu...
Abdulbaseet yayi dariya tare da kanne mata ido daya😉 ya sami guri ya zauna
shi kuma ya ci gaba da yiwa mommy rada...
Jin farooq ya hayayako ta yasa ta koma tayi lamo, wayar ta da ke charji tayi ringing tace da bintu tayi sauri ta miko mata...
Tana dauka taga my love mai gida ya kira ta dan gyara murya ta dauka tare da fadin helow
Jin albishir din nashi yasa ta chanza salon magana 
Allah Toh shikenan allah ya kawo ku lafiya....
A dadai lokacin da najeeb ya shigo, cikin farin cikin yara wa kuke son gani, bintu tayi saurin cewa anty  salamaaaaaaa......::!!!

Duk wannan abubuwa da ke faruwa da wanda suka faru, babu wanda salama tasani, Tana gidan yaya ado sai faman kula take da farida domin kuwa wannan cikin da laulayi mai naci ne, ga farida da san jiki ga kuma rakin ciwo, hakan yasa salama keta hidima da ita.
Lokacin da taji labarin Abdulbaseet zai koma maiduguri kuwa, taso zuwa gida tarar sa ama hakan ya gagara kasancewar bata jin dadi, ita dai zancen ta Yaya Abdulbaseet Yaya Abdulbaseet yaushe zai zo gidan ta yaushe zai zo dai kullun sai ta ishi yaya ado da zancen, sai dai shi tana dinsa bai wuce na kar ta bari salama taji wannan zancen dan da alama bata sani ba....
Yau watan su guda kenan rabon ta da taji daga Abdulbaseet, rabon ta da Abdulbaseet tun ranar da sukayi waya tayi masa gaisuwa gwaggo,,,, tun ranar har yanzu basu sake waya ba...
Zancen gaskia shine salama ta ruga ta sawa ranta yaya ado yaje ya mayar wa da Abdulbaseet kaya ya kuma ce masa ya rabu da ita, maybe shi yasa Abdulbaseet yake jin nauyin kiranta ko haduwar su, ama bata sa a ranta hakan zai iya zama sanadiyar rabuwar su ba, ita a ganin ta its a matter of time tasan tana son Abdulbaseet Abdulbaseet kuma yana son ta da bazasu iya jure rayuwa ba tare da juna ba, duk wani mafarki yanzu na salama to tabasss baya wuce na Abdulbaseet, tsarin diniya tsarin lahira, rayuwar aure zamantakewa da sauran su babu wanda zai yuwu idan ba Abdulbaseet, saboda haka rasa Abdulbaseet tamkar karshen rayuwa ne yazo, if to say hakan ke shirin faruwa da tuni taji ajikinta.
Ama hankalin ta a kwance yake, tasan Abdulbaseet na cikin koshin lafiya, takuma san babu abun da zai faru sai alheri komawa gidan nan da yayi.

Jin muryarshi yasa tayi saurin cewa
Yaya Abdulbaseettttt

Yaya ado da salama suka dago suka kalle ta a lokaci daya.....

Yaya ado ya saki wide smile dinshi tare da fadin so finally Yaya Abdulbaseet is coming, gaskia bai kamata mu zauna haka ba ya kamata mu shiga kitchen....
Salama da ke binsu da kallo cikin zuciyarta tace tohhh dama aunty farida tana da Yaya Abdulbaseet...

Da yake sun dan dau lokaci basu karaso ba hakan yasa, suka sami lokacin girka abinci na gani na fada, kai kace wasu manyan bakin za'a tara...
Suna cikin yi salama take cewa dama anty farida kina da Yaya Abdulbaseet ban sani ba, ni yaya farooq kadai na sani....
Suka kali juna ta gefe ita da yaya ado sai ta dan maze tace, allah sarki salama, Yaya Abdulbaseet cousin dina ne daya dade baya nan, yana kasar waje sai kwanan nan ya dawo...

Cikin murya kasa kasa tace ABDULBASEET

Dangin ango sunyi musu kara sosai dan da yawan su sun sami halara a wajen kumu, abun dai bai yiwa nabeel dadi ba dan har yau bai samu ya ke bikin Abdulbaseet da duk Bodado tasa kifaya babu maza.
Masu kidan qwarya nayi su bawa Dj shima ya kada nashi, anci ansha ,a wajen nan babu wanda ya halarci wannan rana  bai fito da tunbinshi a cike ba da kuma jakar sobeniya ba. Haka tari ya watse sai sa abarka akeyi....

Bayan shekara daya
Shagalin bikin sabeeha da nabeel ake tayi, ama me salama tayi nauyi, bata iya zuwa koina domin haihuwa yau ko gobe, inda abun bai mata daidai ba kenan Abdulbaseet ne kawai ya samu ya je musu duka event din, dan a kano akayi bikin, koda tace zata je ma bazata iya ba.
Haka aka yi bikin nabeel da sabeeha salama na gida tana jiran lokaci.
Ga farooq ma an kai kudi shima an tsaida lokaci nan da wata uku...

Chan kuwa hadiza da Engineer sun hakura da na'ima sun fawala wa allah, taje duniya zatayi mata jawabi, alhakin Abdulbaseet shike bin yarta, dan wani bai bi duniya na gashi nata yani duniya.... waiyazubillahi allah kayi mana tsari daga dukanin mai nufin mu da shari...amin
Abdulbaseet baya yarda ya bar side din salama dan yana tsoron kar ya yi mata nisa tazo ta haihu baya nan, baya barinta tayi aikin dari bare na baki, shi ke komai dan har goya ta da yayi da zata yarda, ga EDD dinta yayi har ya wuce ama har yanzu shiru suna tsumaya...
Yau cikin dare ciwo ya ruske ta ba shiri ya dauke ta sai asibiti, kafin yaje ya kira mommy aziza har an sami karuwa, allah ya azurta su da santalelen da namiji...
Masha allah ubangiji allah ya raya yasa cikon musulimci ne🙏🏼
Kai kar ku so kuga murna wajen Abdulbaseet, kamar ya tashi sama, yau ya zama daddy sa salama ta zama mommy
Ranar suna yaro yaci sunnan mahaifin salama wato Muhammad wanda sukayi wa lakani da Gaddafi (muhammad Gaddafi) 
Anan tayi jegon ta gidan mommy aziza domin lokacin yaya ado da Bodado sun tafi umara, haka aka sha shagali akayi suna na gani na fada, kowa sai murna yake.
Allah ubangiji ya raya Muhammad Gaddafi 🙏🏼

Muhammad Gaddafi nada wata tara a duniya suka kamala shirin tafiya kasar England 🏴󠁧󠁢󠁥󠁮󠁧󠁿 
Sun yi komai sun gama shirya komai, sun je kano sunyi wa Bodado salama, da Engineer da hadiza, suka biya ta gidan nabeel a kaduna da kuma yaya ado sannan suka dawo...
Dangi duk sai da suka shiga sukayi musu sallama a daren ranar aka yi kota guda wajen yi musu rakiya airport.
Abdulbaseet na rike da Muhammad Gaddafi a hannu salama sai yan hawaye take yi yasa matarsa da dansa a gaba suka keta hazo ✈✈✈✈✈✈
